BUSINESS REVIEW AND OUTLOOK                                             業務回顧及展望

In 2024, the macro-economy continued to face downward pressure          2024 年 ， 宏 觀 經 濟 繼 續 面 臨 下 行 壓 力 ，
with consumer demand remaining weak. The paper manufacturing            消費需求疲弱。中國造紙行業在消化過剩
industry in China, however, while absorbing excessive capacity and      產能、木漿價格持續震盪中展現韌性，得
pulp price fluctuations, demonstrated resilience. Benefitting from a    益於政府一系列降準減息、提振消費等刺
series of government stimulation policies such as cutting reserve       激政策，下游需求逐步回暖，造紙企業整
requirement ratio, lowering interest rate, and boosting consumption,    體於年內有所修復。
downstream demand gradually warmed up, resulting in a general
recovery of paper manufacturing companies during the year.


For 2024, the Group recorded total revenue of HK$26.0 billion,          本集團2024年總收入比去年上升4.2%至
representing an increase of 4.2% from that of the previous year,        260億港元，年度盈利較去年上升 17.6%
while the profit for the year increased by 17.6% to HK$1.4 billion as   至 14 億 港 元 。 年 內 每 股 盈 利 30.56 港 仙
compared with that for the previous year. The earnings per share for    （2023年：24.39港仙）。
the year were HK30.56 cents (2023: HK24.39 cents).


The board of directors has proposed a final dividend of HK4.5 cents     董事會建議2024年派發末期股息每股4.5
per share for 2024 (2023: HK6.1 cents).                                 港仙（2023年：6.1港仙）。


INDUSTRY AND BUSINESS REVIEW                                            行業及業務回顧

Looking back at 2024, with new pulp production capacity being           回 顧 2024 年 ， 國 內 新 增 木 漿 產 能 釋 放 ，
released in mainland China, pulp price remained unstable generally.     木漿價格整體維持震盪走勢。造紙行業方
Regarding the paper manufacturing industry, although players            面，雖然年內紙企去庫存速度加快，然而
speeded up destocking during the year, market demand continued          市場需求依然疲弱，加上年內美國大選帶
to be weak. In addition, as the U.S. presidential election during the   來許多不明朗因素，影響整體行業表現。
year brought uncertainties, the industry’s overall performance was
affected.


Facing changes in market supply and demand, the Group insisted on       面對市場的供需變化，本集團堅持積極推
actively promoting integration of its industrial chain to ensure raw    動產業鏈一體化，以確保原材料供應，控
materials supply and control costs, and ultimately improve gross        制成本以改善毛利，同時靈活調節製漿及
profit. By flexibly adjusting the production and sales between pulp     造紙業務之間的生產和銷售，提升營運效
and paper manufacturing businesses, we were able to enhance             益，有效推動整體盈利穩步增長。
operational efficiency and effectively drive steady growth of
profitability overall.

BUSINESS PROSPECTS                                                      業務前景

Looking ahead at 2025, while ongoing Sino-U.S. trade tension is         展 望 2025 年 ， 中 美 貿 易 戰 持 續 緊 張 為 企
casting a shadow over the development prospects of enterprises,         業的發展前景蒙上陰霾，而中國政府強調
new opportunities have emerged at the PRC government policy –           「出口轉內銷」政策則帶來新的機遇。中國
“export goods for domestic sales”. China has clearly proposed earlier   此前明確提出大力提振消費、提高投資效
to vigorously boost consumption and enhance investment efficiency       益，以全方位擴大國內需求為戰略目標。
for realizing the strategic goal of expanding overall domestic          預計相關刺激政策將促進消費支出，持續
demand. Such stimulus policies are expected to help promote             改善消費環境，帶動紙品需求的上升，加
consumer spending, continuously improve consumption sentiment,          上造紙業將進入新增產能週期的尾聲，供
in turn drive increase in demand for paper products. Moreover, as       應預期將逐漸平穩，行業有望迎來供需平
the paper manufacturing industry is close to the end of the capacity    衡的修復及溫和增長。
release cycle, supply is expected to gradually stabilize, meaning the
industry should see the supply-demand balance restore and moderate
growth in its performance.


The Group will seize the opportunities brought about by favorable       本集團將把握利好政策帶來的機遇，積極
policies to actively promote development of its domestic sales          推動內銷業務發展。本集團將透過加強技
business. It will, via pursuing technological upgrades, push to         術改造，提升造紙設備的生產效率，進一
improve production efficiency of its paper-making equipment, aiming     步減低成本、改善利潤率，亦會加強產品
to further reduce costs and widen profit margins. It will also          研發，配合客戶對產品的不同需求，增加
strengthen product research and development to match the diverse        產品的市場競爭力。
needs of customers, thereby bolster the market competitiveness of its
products.

Vietnam to improve overall operational efficiency.                        效率。

paper packaging business, the State Council issued earlier a series of    國務院相繼發布一系列利好紙質包裝業務

the Control of Excessive Packaging of Commodities (《關於進一步加                裝治理的通知》和《國務院辦公廳關於印發
強商品過度包裝治理的通知》) and the Issued Circular of the General                     「十四五」現代物流發展規劃的通知》等，

continued to promote development of the packaging paper business.

of pursuing high quality and environmental friendliness, we have          環保理念，為市場提供天然竹纖維原色衛

Anti-corruption                                                               反貪污

Anti-bribery, Anti-fraud and Anti-money Laundering                            防止賄賂、欺詐及洗黑錢
The Group attaches great importance to integrity supervision, and is          本集團高度重視廉潔監管，致力防範貪
committed to preventing corruption, bribery, fraud and other                  腐、賄賂、欺詐等違法和違反商業道德的
behaviors that are illegal and against commercial ethics, to make sure        行為，確保營運全程遵守所有防止商業罪
we have always observed laws and regulations on prevention of                 案 有 關 的 法 例 法 規 ， 包 括 及 不 限 於《 中 華
commercial crimes in our daily operations, including but not limited          人 民 共 和 國 刑 法 》、《 中 華 人 民 共 和 國 反
to the “Criminal Law of the People’s Republic of China”, the “Anti-           洗 錢 法 》、《 中 華 人 民 共 和 國 反 不 正 當 競
Money Laundering Law of the People’s Republic of China”, the                  爭 法 》、《 2009 年 馬 來 西 亞 反 貪 污 委 員 會
“Anti-Unfair Competition Law of the People’s Republic of China”               法令》等。法規要求企業需遵循平等、公
and the “Malaysian Anti-Corruption Commission Act 2009” and so                平、誠信等原則，依法而行，堅守商業道
forth. Enterprises are required under laws and regulations to abide by        德，不得以盜竊、賄賂、欺詐、脅迫或者
the principles of fairness, equality and integrity, to act in a lawful        以其他不正當手段經營。
manner, to adhere to commercial ethics, and not to operate business
by means of theft, bribery, deception, compulsion or other improper
means.


The Group believes that to achieve anti-corruption, it relies the             本集團相信實現反貪污有賴多方內外合
cooperation of both internal and external parties within the                  作。因此，所有員工亦需要遵守本集團編
organization. Therefore, all employees are required to abide by the           製的《商業道德規範》及《反貪污政策》要求
“Standards for Commercial Ethics” and “Anti-Corruption Policy”                員工以專業合規、平等公正、誠信正直的
complied by the Group, which restricts employees to serve the                 行為服務公司和客戶。本集團的員工守則
Company and customers with professional compliance, equality,                 中亦明文規定了個人及專業操守以期嚴格
fairness, and integrity. The Worker’s Manual of the Group also clearly        制約、防範交易過程出現任何特殊待遇，
stipulates code of personal and professional conduct for their                包括但不限於回扣、昂貴禮品、奢侈款待
stringent compliance, pursuant to which employees are not allowed             等，以免影響其客觀專業的判斷。同時，
to accept any special privilege during transactions, including but not        本 集 團 員 工 在 入 職 時 均 須 簽 署《 廉 潔 從 業
limited to rebates, expensive gifts, luxury hospitality, etc., so as not to   承 諾 書 》， 承 諾 不 可 以 利 用 工 作 職 權 上 的
affect their objectivity and professional judgment. In addition, all          便利為自己索取任何好處，不得為牟取自
employees of the Group are required to sign “Undertaking to                   身利益而損害公司權益。
Integrity” upon onboarding, undertaking that he/she will not use his/

of integrity, the human resources department and the legal
department regularly provide anti-corruption trainings to the
management and employees, and new comers are required to

Health and Safety                                                        健康與安全

As the Group’s production procedures involve the use of machinery        本集團的生產工序中涉及機械使用及較高
and the operation with high risks, we have attached great importance     危操作，因此我們非常重視員工的職業安
to the occupational safety of our employees, and have taken all          全，並採取一切適當措施保障員工在工作
appropriate measures to safeguard the health and safety of our           場所內的健康與安全。本集團的安全工作
workplaces. The safety work of the Group is primarily governed           主 要 受 中 國 內 地 的《 中 華 人 民 共 和 國 安 全
under the “Law of Production Safety of the People’s Republic of          生 產 法 》、《 中 華 人 民 共 和 國 職 業 病 防 治
China”, the “Law of the Prevention and Control of Occupational           法 》和 馬 來 西 亞 的《 2022 年 職 業 安 全 與 健
Diseases of the People’s Republic of China”, the “Occupational           康（修訂）法》及其他相關的安全法規、標
Safety and Health (Amendment) Act 2022” of Malaysia and other            準和制度監管，強調安全生產的重要性，
relevant safety regulations, standards and systems. The regulations      保障員工的生命和財產安全。我們以法規
emphasised that production safety is important for the safety of         中的「安全第一，預防為主，綜合治理」的
employees’ lives and property. We shall take the objectives of “Safety   方針為綱領，建立了完善的安全生產及職
First, with Emphasis on Prevention and Integrated Governance” as         業病防治責任制，並設立安全管理規章制
our guiding principles to establish a sound system of accountability     度，有效地經營安全生產工作。本集團主
for production safety and the prevention and control of occupational     要從以下五方面貫徹法例法規要求：
diseases, and to form policies and procedures for safety management
with a view to be effectively engaged in production safety. The
Group complies with the requirements under laws and regulations in
five ways as follows:

Setting Up Environmental Team
成立環保小組

Led by our Chief Executive Officer, the environmental team comprises members having the relevant expertise,
including senior flue gas treatment experts, sewage treatment technicians, environmental monitoring professionals
and environmental protection technicians so as to ensure effective implementation of the following measures.
由首席執行官親自帶領，由合資格專才組成，包括高級煙氣治理師、污水處理人員、環境監測人員、環境保護專業
技術人員等，保證下列相關環保措施得以有效執行。

Duties of Environmental Team
環保小組職責

1.   Holding internal control meetings regularly to review environmental issues in the plants, to update themselves
     with applicable environmental laws and regulations and to give advice on any improvements;
2.   On an ongoing basis, the team engages themselves in environmental protection measures to be taken in
     production processes, including those employed to lower water consumption, treat sewage water and air
     emissions more efficiently, save energy and monitoring emissions to ensure continued compliance with high
     environmental protection standards.
1.   定期召開內部監控會議，旨在審閱有關廠房內的環保事宜，通知各成員環保法律及規定的更新條文，並為存
     在改善空間的環節提供適當建議；
2.   不斷完善生產過程中的環保措施，包括減少耗水量、應用更有效的廢水處理技術、節省能源耗用量、採用更
     環保的廢氣處理技術及排放監控等，確保可持續地達到高環保標準。

✓    set up policies for emissions treatment to provide clear targets and state the responsibilities and division of
     labour for environmental protection measures adopted by the Group;
✓    formulate guidelines for energy-saving and reduce emission at the source;
✓    as part of the various efforts to make us one of the companies which best complies with environmental
     protection principles in the world, our different plants have employed technologies with high efficiency to
     reduce emission and keep our emission below the latest emission standards laid down by the government as
     well as internationally applicable environmental management standards.
✓    設立排放物處理辦法，為本集團採納之環保措施提供明確的目標和職責分工；

                              Case Study: Environmentally friendly Thermal Power Station
                                               個案研究：環保熱電站

Environmentally friendly circulating fluidised bed boilers used in our
thermal power stations can achieve efficient combustion of all
kinds of coal. As the combustion temperature in this kind of boilers
is lower than that in pulverised coal boilers, less nitrogen oxides
would be generated.
本集團的熱電站一致採用環保型的循環流化床鍋爐，可以有效
焚燒各煤種，該種鍋爐燃燒溫度低於煤粉爐，產生的氮氧化物
排放較少。


In each coal-fired power station, the most advanced air emissions
treatment facilities have been installed, including the employment
of highly efficient bag filters to reduce ashes, SCR/SNCR de-
nitration systems to eliminate nitrogen oxides, and limestone-
gypsum flue gas desulfurisation system to curb sulphur dioxide gas.
We have successfully reduced smoke and sulphur by more than
99.9% and 90% respectively.
每個燃煤發電廠均配備並使用最先進的廢氣處理設施，包括高
效布袋除塵器去除煙塵；SCR/SNCR脫硝法去除氮氧化物；石
灰石膏脫硫法去除二氧化硫等。目前煙塵去除率已達99.9%以
上，脫硫率達90%以上。                                                                  Circulating Fluidised Bed Boilers
                                                                                     循環流化床鍋爐
                                                                                                                   3
Our effective wet desulfurisation facility further reduced sulphur dioxide emissions to much less than 400 mg/m as
required by the “Emission Standard of Air Pollutants for Thermal Power Plants” (GB13223-2011) in China. The Group
has monitored the quality of air emissions on a real-time basis by using online detection equipment to ensure
outperforming the relevant national standard.
此外，我們高效的濕法脫硫措施，進一步把二氧化硫排放量降至遠低於國家GB13223-2011《火電廠大氣污染
物排放標準》要求的每立方米400毫克標準。本集團通過在線監察設備實時監測排放廢氣的質量，確保排放高
於國家規定的標準。


Among which, the combustion system of No. 1-4 circulating fluidised bed boilers of Guangdong Lee & Man were
optimized and reformed by adopting the LNB technology to reduce the raw emission of nitrogen oxides from boiler
                         3
flue gas to 110-130mg/Nm , so as to lay a solid foundation for the realization of ultra-low emission of nitrogen oxides
from boiler flue gas.
其中廣東理文的1-4號循環流體化床鍋爐進行燃燒系統優化改造，採用LNB技術，降低鍋爐煙氣氮氧化物原始
排放至110-130mg/Nm 3 ，為鍋爐煙氣氮氧化物實施超低 排放打好基礎。


No. 1-3 boilers adopted the PNCR+SNCR combined denitration technology, which effectively improved
the denitration efficiency and achieved the ultra-low emission standard of nitrogen oxides from boiler
flue gas of ≤50mg/Nm 3 .
1-3號鍋爐更採用PNCR+SNCR合併脫硝技術，有效提升脫硝效率，達到鍋爐菸氣氮氧化物超低排放標準，
≤50mg/Nm 3 。

Wastes                                                                      廢棄物
The wastes generated in our operations were mainly non-hazardous            本集團營運所產生的廢物主要為造紙過程
ones produced in the course of paper-making, together with a small          中所產生的無害廢棄物，亦有少量日常行
quantity of wastes due to routine office work. In order to reduce and       政辦公所產生的廢棄物。針對造紙過程中
dispose of waste produced in the paper-making process, the Group            所產生的廢棄物的減少和處理，本集團已
has established the “Guidelines for Waste Management” to classify           設立《廢棄物管理指導書》對本集團所有活
wastes of all activities and clarify the workflow of processing. Policies   動產生的廢棄物進行分類，明確其處理操
also provide for guidelines for the storage, transport and disposal of      作流程。政策亦為廢棄物的存放、運輸及
wastes and require them to be recorded to ensure that treatment             處置提供指引並要求記錄在案，確保處理
process is in compliance with rules and is safe.                            過程安全合規。

The Group has principally disposed of wastes generated from its             本集團主要通過以下三種方式處理廢棄
paper-making operations in three ways:                                      物：

•     Sewage treatment facilities in plants will filter sewage              •   工廠的污水處理設施過濾造紙過程
      generated from the paper-making process. This process would               中所產生的污水，平均每月會產生
      generate an average of over 10,000 tonnes of sludge. It is then           過萬噸污泥。污泥經處理及深度脫
      burnt in boilers after treatment and deep drying. The energy              水後被送至鍋爐焚燒，通過迴圈流
      generated by burning the waste in a circulating fluidised bed             化床鍋爐焚燒的廢物所產生的能量
      boilers could be reused in the paper-making process and                   用於造紙生產，不但不會造成二次
      therefore, not only secondary pollution would be avoided but              污染，還節省了能源消耗；
      also saving energy;

•     The Group used lots of waste paper as a raw material in its           •   本集團在造紙生產過程中使用大量
      paper-making operations, paper-making residue was left                    廢紙作為原料，廢紙經提取有用的
      behind after extraction of useful fibres for the paper-making             造紙纖維後剩下造紙廢渣。本集團
      process. As the residue contained different impurities, the               把造紙廢渣引進洗水線提取有價值

      wires and plastics in the residue were collected and sold to              物料出售予回收商進行回收再造；
      recyclers;

                              Case Study: Cases of Guangdong Lee & Man and Malaysia Lee & Man
                                     – Energy Comprehensive Utilization of Residue Project
                               個案研究：廣東理文及馬來西亞理文個案－廢渣能源綜合利用項目

                               12
Guangdong Lee & Man : The project builds a residue
boiler – turbine generator set that waste residue, waste
plastic, sludge and biogas generated from the paper
production line as fuel to generate steam and electricity for
the boiler fuel; it can reduce 340,000 tonnes of industrial
solid waste, generate 163 million kWh of electricity and
630,000 tonnes of steam in a year, which is equivalent to
a reduction of 106,000 tonnes of raw coal.
廣東理文 12 ：工程建置廢渣鍋爐－汽輪發電機組，以造
紙 生 產 線 產 生 的 廢 渣 、 廢 塑 膠 、 污 泥 及 沼 氣為燃料，
為鍋爐燃料產生蒸汽和電力；一年可減少34萬噸工業
固體廢棄物，發電量1.63億度，產生蒸汽63萬噸；相
當減少原煤10.6萬噸。

                         13
Malaysia Lee & Man : The project builds a residue boiler –
turbine generator set that waste residue, pulp residue and
sludge generated from the paper production line as fuel to
generate steam and electricity for the boiler fuel; it can
reduce 115,600 tonnes of industrial solid waste, generate
100 million kWh of electricity and 470,000 tonnes of
steam in a year, which is equivalent to a reduction of
37,000 tonnes of raw coal, with obvious environmental
and economic benefits.
馬來西亞理文 13 ：工程建置廢渣鍋爐－汽輪發電機組，
以 造 紙 生 產 線 產 生 的 廢 渣 、 漿 渣 及 污 泥 為 燃 料，為鍋
爐燃料產生蒸汽和電力；一年可減少11.56萬噸工業固
體廢棄物，發電量1億度，產生蒸汽47萬噸；相當減少
原煤3.7萬噸，環保及經濟效益明顯。

                    Case Study: Chongqing Lee & Man – Solid Waste Incinerator Production Line
                                   個案研究：重慶理文－固廢焚燒爐生產線


A solid waste incinerator production line was put into
operation with production capacity of 1,000 tonnes per
day by Chongqing Lee & Man in 2024, generating a total
of 71,240 MWh of electricity and 410,000 tonnes of high-
pressure steam. At the same time, the production line can
reduce the consumption of raw coal by 31,000 tonnes,
reduce carbon emissions by 49,000 tonnes, and incinerate
paper solid waste and sludge from the sewage collection
points, reducing solid waste emissions by 115,000 tonnes.
重 慶 理 文 於 2024 年 投 產 一 條 每 日 1,000 噸 之 固 廢 焚 燒
爐生產線，投產後共計發電71,240兆瓦時，產生高壓
蒸汽41萬噸，同時減少原煤消耗3.1萬噸，減少碳排放
量4.9萬噸，造紙廢渣及污水站污泥入爐燃燒，減少固
體廢物排放量11.5萬噸。

USE OF RESOURCES                                                                           資源使用

With sustainable development as the prerequisite, the Group sets                           本集團以可持續發展為前提，訂立多項資
goals for resource consumption to protect environment, to make                             源降耗目標，致力於保護環境，合理科學
rational and scientific use of energy and resources and to reduce                          地使用能源及資源，並降低生產成本。本
production costs. The Group has formulated the “Guide for Energy-                          集 團 編 製 了《 節 約 能 源 資 源 控 制 管 理 指 導
saving and Control and Management of Resources” to regulate the                            書》以規範各活動中能源及資源的使用。
use of energy and resources in various activities. The policy specifies                    該政策統一釐定了各部門的職責及分工，
the responsibilities of and division of labour among departments,                          生產相關部門包括採購部、生產部、維修
whereby departments related to production including the                                    車間及設備使用部門等均需控制原材料、
procurement department, the production department and                                      設備、水、電、紙、包裝物料等能源及資
departments using maintenance workshops and facilities are required                        源的耗用，而行政部門包括人事部、行政
to control the consumption of energy and resources such as raw                             部、後勤部等則需負責對本集團全員進行

financial statements and our auditor’s report thereon.

the other information and we do not express any form of assurance         資料，本行亦不對該等其他資料發表任何
conclusion thereon.                                                       形式的鑒證結論。


In connection with our audit of the consolidated financial statements,    結合本行對綜合財務報表的審計，本行的
our responsibility is to read the other information and, in doing so,     責任是閱讀其他資料，在此過程中，考慮
consider whether the other information is materially inconsistent with    其他資料是否與綜合財務報表或本行在審
the consolidated financial statements or our knowledge obtained in        計過程中所了解的情況存在重大抵觸或者
the audit or otherwise appears to be materially misstated. If, based      似乎存在重大錯誤陳述的情況。基於本行
on the work we have performed, we conclude that there is a material       已執行的工作，如果本行認為其他資料存
misstatement of this other information, we are required to report         在重大錯誤陳述，本行需要報告該事實。
that fact. We have nothing to report in this regard.                      在這方面，本行沒有任何報告。

     the notes to the financial statements and improve aggregation              註中提供管理層界定績效衡量的披
     and disaggregation of information to be disclosed in the                   露，並改善財務報表中呈列的匯總
     financial statements. In addition, some HKAS 1 paragraphs                  及分類資料。此外，香港會計準則
     have been moved to HKAS 8 Accounting Policies, Changes in                  第1號若干段落已移至香港會計準則
     Accounting Estimates and Errors and HKFRS 7 Financial                      第8號會計政策、會計估計變動及錯
     Instruments: Disclosures . Minor amendments to HKAS 7                      誤 及香港財務報告準則第7號金融工
     Statement of Cash Flows and HKAS 33 Earnings per Share are                 具：披露 。香港會計準則第7號現金

                                                                                盈利 亦有輕微修訂。

3. BASIS OF PREPARATION OF CONSOLIDATED                                   3.   綜合財務報表的編製基準及
   FINANCIAL STATEMENTS AND MATERIAL                                           重大會計政策資料
   ACCOUNTING POLICIES INFORMATION
      3.1 Basis of preparation of consolidated                                 3.1 綜合財務報表的編製基準
          financial statements

          The consolidated financial statements have been                        綜合財務報表乃根據香港會計
          prepared in accordance with HKFRSs issued by the                       師公會頒布之香港財務報告準
          HKICPA. For the purpose of preparation of the                          則編製。就編製綜合財務報表
          consolidated financial statements, information is                      而言，倘資料可以合理預期會
          considered material if such information is reasonably                  影響主要使用者作出決策，則
          expected to influence decisions made by primary users. In              此等資料被認為重大。此外，
          addition, the consolidated financial statements include                綜合財務報表包括聯交所證券
          applicable disclosures required by the Rules Governing                 上 市 規 則（「 上 市 規 則 」）及 香
          the Listing of Securities on the Stock Exchange (the                   港公司條例規定之適用披露資
          “Listing Rules”) and the Hong Kong Companies                           料。
          Ordinance.


          In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the                於編製綜合財務報表時，本公
          directors of the Company have given careful                            司 董 事 鑑 於 本 集 團 於 2024 年
          consideration to the future liquidity of the Group in light            12月31日之流動負債超出其流
          of the fact that the Group’s current liabilities exceeded its          動資產30,328,000港元事項而
          current assets by HK$30,328,000 as at 31 December                      關注本集團之未來流動資金。
          2024. In the opinion of the directors of the Company,                  本公司董事認為，本集團有數
          the Group has a number of sources of finance available                 項可動用資金來源為其營運提
          to fund its operations, including but not limited to                   供資金，包括但不限於內部資
          internal resources and available unutilised facilities from            源及來自金融機構之未動用授
          financial institutions. Accordingly, the consolidated                  信。據此，綜合財務報表乃以
          financial statements have been prepared on a going                     持續經營基準編製。
          concern basis.

          achieved where the Company:                                            權是指本公司：

                involvement with the investee; and                                    獲得或有權獲得可變回

                                                                                      其回報。

          investee if facts and circumstances indicate that there are            制元素其中一項或多項改變，

          listed above.                                                          然控制被投資方。

          rights of an investee, it has power over the investee when             多數投票權，則本集團僅在投
          the voting rights are sufficient to give it the practical              票權足以賦予其實際能力可
          ability to direct the relevant activities of the investee              單方面指示被投資公司的相關
          unilaterally. The Group considers all relevant facts and               活動時方對被投資公司擁有權
          circumstances in assessing whether or not the Group’s                  力。在評估本集團於被投資公
          voting rights in an investee are sufficient to give it power,          司的投票權是否足以賦予其權
          including:                                                             力時，本集團考慮一切相關事
                                                                                 實及情況，包括：


          •     the size of the Group’s holding of voting rights                 •    本集團所持投票權的規模
                relative to the size and dispersion of holdings of the                相對於其他投票權持有人

          historical cost in a foreign currency are not retranslated.           算。

          neither planned nor likely to occur (therefore forming                淨 投 資 之 一 部 分 ）除 外 ， 該 差

          the end of each reporting period. Income and expenses                 元 ）， 而 收 入 及 支 出 項 目 乃 按

          Borrowing costs                                                       借貸成本
          Borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition,             與購置、建造或生產合資格資
          construction or production of qualifying assets, which are            產（ 需 一 段 長 時 間 方 可 作 擬 定
          assets that necessarily take a substantial period of time to          用 途 或 可 供 銷 售 的 資 產 ）直 接
          get ready for their intended use or sale, are added to the            有關之借貸成本均加至該等資
          cost of those assets until such time as the assets are                產之成本，直至該等資產大致
          substantially ready for their intended use or sale.                   可作其擬定用途或可供銷售為
                                                                                止。


          Any specific borrowing that remain outstanding after the              於相關資產可用於擬定用途或
          related asset is ready for its intended use or sale is                出售後仍未償還的任何特定借
          included in the general borrowing pool for calculation of             貸計入一般借貸池，以計算一
          capitalisation rate on general borrowings.                            般借貸的資本化率。


          All other borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss            所有其他借貸成本均於產生期
          in the period in which they are incurred.                             間於損益中確認。


          Government grants                                                     政府補助
          Government grants are not recognised until there is                   政府補助於合理保證本集團將
          reasonable assurance that the Group will comply with the              遵守其附帶的條件以及將收到
          conditions attaching to them and that the grants will be              補助的情況下方會予以確認。
          received.


          Government grants related to income that are receivable               與收入有關的政府補助，乃對
          as compensation for expenses or losses already incurred               已產生的費用或虧損，或向本
          or for the purpose of giving immediate financial support              集團提供即時的財政支持而
          to the Group with no future related costs are recognised              並無未來的相關成本之應收補
          in profit or loss in the period in which they become                  償，於變為應收期間在損益中
          receivable. Such grants are presented under “other                    確 認 。 有 關 補 助 呈 列 於「 其 他
          income”.                                                              收入」項下。

                                                                                 率計算。

          that taxable profits will be available against which those             使用該可扣稅暫時差額時確
          deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such                 認。倘暫時差額乃由初始確認

          present value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects             量乃使用稅前貼現率折現至其
          current market assessments of the time value of money                 貼現值，該貼現率反映目前市
          and the risks specific to the asset (or a cash-generating             場對資金時間值之評估以及估
          unit) for which the estimates of future cash flows have               計未來現金流量未經調整之資
          not been adjusted.                                                    產（或現金產生單位）的特定風
                                                                                險。


          If the recoverable amount of an asset (or a cash-                      倘某項資產（或現金產生單位）
          generating unit) is estimated to be less than its carrying             之可收回金額估計低於其賬面
          amount, the carrying amount of the asset (or a cash-                   值 ， 則 該 項 資 產（ 或 現 金 產 生
          generating unit) is reduced to its recoverable amount. In              單 位 ）之 賬 面 值 須 削 減 至 其 可
          allocating the impairment loss, the impairment loss is                 收回金額。於分配減值虧損
          allocated first to reduce the carrying amount of any                   時，首先分配減值虧損以減少
          goodwill (if applicable) and then to the other assets on a             任何商譽（倘適用）之賬面值，
          pro-rata basis based on the carrying amount of each asset              然後根據該單位中每項資產之
          in the unit. The carrying amount of an asset is not                    賬面值按比例分配予其他資
          reduced below the highest of its fair value less costs of              產。某項資產之賬面值不會削
          disposal (if measurable), its value in use (if determinable)           減至低於其公允值減去出售成
          and zero. The amount of the impairment loss that would                 本（ 倘 可 計 量 ）、 其 使 用 價 值
          otherwise have been allocated to the asset is allocated               （ 倘 可 釐 定 ）及 零 三 者 之 最 高
          pro rata to the other assets of the unit. An impairment                者。原本應分配至該資產之減
          loss is recognised immediately in profit or loss.                      值虧損金額按比例分配予該單
                                                                                 位之其他資產。減值虧損即時
                                                                                 在損益中確認。

          The Group performs impairment assessment under                          本集團於金融資產就預期信貸
          expected credit loss (“ECL”) model on financial assets                  虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）模式進
          which are subject to impairment assessment under HKFRS                  行減值評估乃視乎香港財務準
          9 (including trade and other receivables, loan to a joint               則第9號項下的減值評估（包括
          venture, amounts due from related companies and bank                    應收貿易及其他賬款、合營企
          balances). The amount of ECL is updated at each                         業貸款、應收關連公司款項及
          reporting date to reflect changes in credit risk since initial          銀 行 結 餘 ）。 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 金
          recognition.                                                            額於每個報告日期更新以反映
                                                                                  自初始確認以來之信貸風險變
                                                                                  動。


          Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from all               終身預期信貸虧損指由所有可
          possible default events over the expected life of the                   能違約事件超出相關工具預期
          relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m                    壽命將引致之預期信貸虧損。
          ECL”) represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is                    相 反 ， 12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損
          expected to result from default events that are possible                （「 12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 」）指
          within 12 months after the reporting date. Assessment is                終身預期信貸虧損預期從可能
          done based on the Group’s historical credit loss                        於報告日期後12個月內的違約
          experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the               事件引致之部分。所作評估基
          debtors, general economic conditions and an assessment                  於本集團過往信貸虧損記錄，
          of both the current conditions at the reporting date as                 並調整債務人的特定因素，普
          well as the forecast of future conditions.                              遍經濟狀況及同時對於報告日
                                                                                  期之現況及預測未來狀況之評
                                                                                  估。

          The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for trade                      本集團通常就應收貿易賬款確
          receivables.                                                            認終身預期信貸虧損。


          For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss                  就所有其他工具，本集團以相
          allowance equal to 12m ECL, unless there has been a                     等 於 12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 計
          significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,          量虧損撥備，除非自初始確認
          in which case the Group recognises lifetime ECL. The                    後信貸風險顯著增加，本集團
          assessment of whether lifetime ECL should be recognised                 則確認終身預期信貸虧損。評
          is based on significant increases in the likelihood or risk             估是否應確認終身預期信貸虧
          of a default occurring since initial recognition.                       損乃基於自初始確認後發生違
                                                                                  約的可能性或風險是否顯著增
                                                                                  加。


          (i)   Significant increase in credit risk                               (i)   信貸風險顯著增加


                In assessing whether the credit risk has increased                      於評估自初始確認後信貸
                significantly since initial recognition, the Group                      風險是否顯著增加時，本
                compares the risk of a default occurring on the                         集團將於報告日期金融工
                financial instrument as at the reporting date with                      具發生違約之風險與於初
                the risk of a default occurring on the financial                        始確認日期金融工具發生
                instrument as at the date of initial recognition. In                    違約之風險作比較。作出
                making this assessment, the Group considers both                        評估時，本集團同時考慮
                quantitative and qualitative information that is                        合理及有理據的定量及定
                reasonable and supportable, including historical                        性資料，包括過往經驗及
                experience and forward-looking information that is                      無需花費成本或精力而可
                available without undue cost or effort.                                 得之前瞻性資料。

                 significantly since initial recognition when contractual                 30 天 ， 本 集 團 即 假 定 信

                                                                                          明。


          (ii)   Definition of default                                             (ii)   違約的定義


                 For internal credit risk management, the Group                           就內部信貸風險管理，當
                 considers an event of default occurs when                                內部開發資料或取得外部
                 information developed internally or obtained from                        資料顯示債務人不太可能
                 external sources indicates that the debtor is unlikely                   全數支付其債權人，包括
                 to pay its creditors, including the Group, in full                       本 集 團（ 並 未 考 慮 本 集 團
                 (without taking into account any collaterals held by                     持 有 任 何 抵 押 品 ）， 本 集
                 the Group).                                                              團認為發生違約事件。


                 Irrespective of the above, the Group considers that                      不管上述情況如何，當金
                 default has occurred when a financial asset is more                      融 資 產 逾 期 超 過 90 天 ，
                 than 90 days past due unless the Group has                               本集團認為已發生違約事
                 reasonable and supportable information to                                件，除非本集團具合理及
                 demonstrate that a more lagging default criterion is                     有理據資料顯示滯後違約
                 more appropriate.                                                        標準更為合適。

          (iii)   Credit-impaired financial assets                               (iii)   信貸減值金融資產


                  A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or                       當一項或多項違約事件發
                  more events of default that have a detrimental                         生令金融資產估計未來現
                  impact on the estimated future cash flows of that                      金流量具不利影響，該金
                  financial asset have occurred.                                         融資產則信貸減值。


          (iv)    Write-off policy                                               (iv)    撇銷政策


                  The Group writes off a financial asset when there is                   當有資料顯示對手方存在
                  information indicating that the counterparty is in                     嚴重財政困難及並無實際
                  severe financial difficulty and there is no realistic                  把握收回款項，例如，當
                  prospect of recovery, for example, when the                            對手方已進行清盤或已
                  counterparty has been placed under liquidation or                      進入破產程序，或如為
                  has entered into bankruptcy proceedings, or in the                     應收貿易賬款，當金額乃
                  case of trade receivables, when the amounts are                        逾期超過兩年，以較早者
                  over two years past due, whichever occurs sooner.                      為準，本集團撇銷金融資
                  Financial assets written off may still be subject to                   產。金融資產的撇銷可能
                  enforcement activities under the Group’s recovery                      仍須根據本集團回收程序
                  procedures, taking into account legal advice where                     的執行方式，當適用時考
                  appropriate. A write-off constitutes a derecognition                   慮法律建議。撇銷構成剔
                  event. Any subsequent recoveries are recognised in                     除確認事項。任何其後回
                  profit or loss.                                                        撥於損益確認。

          (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                              (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確
                                                                                      認


                The measurement of ECL is a function of the                           預期信貸虧損計量具違約
                probability of default, loss given default (i.e. the                  可 能 性 ， 默 認 虧 損（ 即 違
                magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and the                  約 的 損 失 幅 度 ）及 違 約 曝
                exposure at default. The assessment of the                            光的功能。違約可能性及
                probability of default and loss given default is based                默認虧損的評估乃基於過
                on historical data and forward-looking information.                   往數據及前瞻性資料。預
                Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and                            期信貸虧損的估計反映不
                probability-weighted amount that is determined                        偏倚及概率加權金額乃按
                with the respective risks of default occurring as the                 發生違約相關風險的比重
                weights.                                                              來釐定。


                Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                      一般而言，預期信貸虧損
                contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in                   為根據合約所有應付本集
                accordance with the contract and the cash flows                       團合約現金流量與本集團
                that the Group expects to receive, discounted at                      於初始確認時預期收到，
                the effective interest rate determined at initial                     按實際利率折現的現金流
                recognition.                                                          量的差額。

          loss is recognised in profit or loss on the purchase, sale,           出售、發行及註銷本公司自身

          instruments.                                                          虧。

     described in note 3, the directors of the Company are required          註 3 ）時 ， 本 公 司 董 事 需 對 未 能 透 過

     carrying amounts of assets and liabilities that are not readily         賬面值作出判斷、估計及假設。該
     apparent from other sources. The estimates and associated               等估計及相關假設乃建基於過往經
     underlying assumptions are based on historical experience and           驗及其他被視為相關之因素。實際
     other factors that are considered to be relevant. Actual results        結果可能有別於該等估計。
     may differ from these estimates.

     ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised         審閱。如對會計估計之修訂僅影響
     in the period in which the estimate is revised if the revision          進行修訂之期間，則於該期間確認

     periods.

19. INVESTMENT PROPERTIES                                                    19. 投資物業

     The Group leases out commercial and industrial properties                 本集團根據經營租賃按每月付租出
     under operating leases with rentals payable monthly. The leases           租商業及工業物業。租賃通常初始
     typically run for an initial period of 1 to 10 years, with unilateral     年期為1至10年，承租人單方面有權
     rights to extend the lease beyond initial period held by lessees          延續租賃至初始年期外。大部分租
     only. Majority of the lease contracts contain annual lease                賃合約在租期內固定年度租賃付款。
     payment that are fixed over the lease term.


     The Group is not exposed to foreign currency risk as a result of          本集團在租賃安排並無承受外幣風
     the lease arrangements, as all leases are denominated in the              險，因所有租賃乃按集團實體相關
     respective functional currencies of group entities. The lease             功能貨幣計值。租賃合約並無包含
     contracts do not contain residual value guarantee and lessee’s            剩餘價值保證及承租人於租期完結
     option to purchase the property at the end of lease term.                 時購入物業之權利。

     in Hong Kong. The assets of the MPF scheme are held                立一項強積金計劃。強積金計劃
     separately from those of the Group, in funds under the control     的資產與本集團的資產分開持有，
     of trustees. The Group contributes monthly the lower of            以及撥入信託人管理的基金。本集
     HK$1,500 or 5% of the relevant monthly payroll costs to the        團 按 1,500 港 元 或 有 關 月 薪 成 本 的
     MPF Scheme, which contribution is matched by employees.            5%，以較低者為準，向強積金計劃
                                                                        供款，與僱員之供款一致。


     The only obligation of the Group with respect of MPF Scheme is     本集團對強積金計劃的唯一責任就
     to make the required contribution payable on the future years.     是在未來年度作出所需應付供款。
     There are no forfeited contributions for the MPF Scheme as the     由於供款於向強積金計劃付款後全
     contributions are fully vested to the employees upon payments      數歸屬予僱員，故強積金計劃並無
     to MPF Scheme.                                                     沒收供款。

     the PRC government. The PRC subsidiaries are required to           計劃。中國附屬公司須按僱員薪金
     contribute a certain percentage of their payroll to the            的若干百分比，向退休福利計劃供

     scheme is to make the specified contributions.                     供款。

35. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                  35. 資本風險管理

      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the             本集團管理其資本以確保本集團的
      Group will be able to continue as a going concern while                  實體可持續經營業務，並透過最佳
      maximising the return to stakeholders through the optimisation           債務及權益比例為權益持有人提供
      of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy             最高回報。本集團的整體政策維持
      remains unchanged from prior year.                                       與去年一致。


      The capital structure of the Group consists of debts, which              本集團的資本結構包括債務，當中
      include the bank borrowings and lease liabilities as disclosed in        包括分別於附註30及28披露之銀行
      notes 30 and 28 respectively, and equity attributable to equity          借貸及租賃負債，以及本公司權益
      holders of the Company, comprising issued share capital,                 持有人應佔權益（包括已發行股本、
      various reserves and perpetual capital securities.                       多項儲備及永續資本證券）。


      The directors of the Company review the capital structure on a           本公司董事不時檢討資本結構。作
      regular basis. As part of this review, the directors consider the        為檢討的一部分，董事考慮資本成
      cost of capital and the risks associated with each class of              本以及與各類資本有關的風險。根
      capital. Based on recommendations of the directors, the Group            據董事的建議，本集團將通過派付
      will balance its overall capital structure through the payment of        股息、發行新股及購回股份以及發
      dividends, new share issues and share buy-backs as well as the           行新債或贖回現債平衡其整體資本
      issue of new debts or the redemption of existing debts.                  結構。

          enhancements to cover its credit risks associated with its            及有票據貸押之應收貿易賬款
          financial assets.                                                     外，本集團並無持有任何抵押

          HKFRS 9 on trade balances individually or based on                    根據預期信貸虧損模式對貿易

          reduced.                                                              信貸風險已大大減少。

          backed by bills is limited because the counterparties are             及國際信用評級機構給予高信

          assigned by international credit-rating agencies.                     有票據質押之應收貿易賬款之
                                                                                信貸風險有限。

          Accordingly, the Group’s concentration of credit risk is              區域。因此，本集團集中信貸
